{\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"John and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Doe\",\"Joe John\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\n\nExample 8b: Adding participants to prompt with NO participants (correction-only)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Doe\",\"Joe John\"]\"}]\nOutput (unchanged - no participants in original, so don't add any in correction-only mode):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\n\nExample 8c: Adding a participant when trailing tokens follow the existing participant (insertion adjacent, NOT appended)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Coffee with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Patrick\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"tomorrow\", \"value\": \"2025-10-18\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Chloe Horgan\"]\"}]\nnow: \"2025-10-17T12:00:00\"\nOutput (new participant inserted ADJACENT to existing participant, BEFORE the startDate — not at the end):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Coffee with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Patrick\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" and \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Chloe Horgan\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"tomorrow\", \"value\": \"2025-10-18\"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: appending \"[ and ][Chloe Horgan]\" after the startDate (\"Coffee with Patrick tomorrow and Chloe Horgan\") — participant cluster must stay contiguous\n\nExample 8d: Replacing a participant via swap (addedParticipants + removedParticipants)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"title\", \"text\": \"brainstorm\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Alex\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Wednesday\", \"value\": \"2025-02-05\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"5pm\", \"value\": \"17:00\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"removedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Alex\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Blake\"]\"}]\nOutput (swap: replace Alex → Blake in-place, preserve \" with \" connector):\n[{\"type\": \"title\", \"text\": \"brainstorm\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Blake\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Wednesday\", \"value\": \"2025-02-05\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"5pm\", \"value\": \"17:00\"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: dropping Alex without adding Blake — \"brainstorm on Wednesday at 5pm\" loses the participant slot entirely\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: dropping the \" with \" text component along with Alex — \"brainstorm Blake Wednesday at 5pm\" loses the connector. The connector text component MUST be preserved verbatim on a swap.\n\nExample 8e: Adding multiple participants to an event with one existing participant (aliasing in original slot)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Coffee with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Patrick\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Adam Kemp\",\"Chloe Horgan\"]\"}]\nOutput (new_total = 3 → PATH A2: REPLACE the \"Patrick\" participant component with a single participantAlias IN THE SAME POSITION; surrounding \" with \" and trailing \" \" text components preserved verbatim):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Coffee with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"Patrick and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"Patrick\",\"Adam Kemp\",\"Chloe Horgan\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: returning the input unchanged (failing to apply the addition)\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: appending \"Adam Kemp\" and \"Chloe Horgan\" as participant components after the trailing \" \" — the alias must occupy the original participant slot, not the end of the array\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: producing three individual participant components \"Patrick\", \"Adam Kemp\", \"Chloe Horgan\" — 3+ total MUST be a single participantAlias\n\nExample 8f: Swap that crosses INTO the alias threshold (all originals removed; primary name from first added)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Coffee with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Patrick\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"removedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Patrick\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Blake\",\"Casey\",\"Drew\"]\"}]\nOutput (final_total = 3 → emit a single participantAlias in Patrick's slot; all originals removed so primary name = first added = \"Blake\"; \" with \" preserved):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Coffee with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"Blake and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"Blake\",\"Casey\",\"Drew\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: keeping \"Patrick\" in the alias text (Patrick was removed)\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: dropping \" with \" (this is a swap; introductory text is preserved)\n\nExample 8g: Swap that crosses OUT of the alias threshold (de-alias to individuals)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"John and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"John\",\"Jane\",\"Joe\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"removedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Jane\",\"Joe\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Sue\"]\"}]\nOutput (final_total = 2 → DE-ALIAS: replace participantAlias with two individual participant components joined by \" and \"; \" with \" preserved):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"John\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" and \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Sue\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: emitting \"John and 1 other\" as a participantAlias (alias requires ≥3 attendees)\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: appending Sue at the end of the array (cluster stays in the alias's original slot)\n\n## Output Format Reference Examples\n\nThe following examples demonstrate correct component structure, type assignments, and value formats for various scenarios. Use these as structural references.\n\nExample R1: Components with participants (3+ attendees use participantAlias)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-20T14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Smith\",\"Joe Johnson\"]\"}]\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Oct 20\", \"value\": \"2025-10-20\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"John Doe and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Smith\",\"Joe Johnson\"]\"}]\n\nExample R2: Components with participants (2 attendees use individual participant components)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-20T14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Smith\"]\"}]\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Oct 20\", \"value\": \"2025-10-20\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"John Doe\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" and \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Jane Smith\"}]\n\nExample R3: Same-day timed event (date omitted, time only)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-17T14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\"]\"}]\nnow: \"2025-10-17T12:00:00\"\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"John Doe\"}]\n\nExample R4: Next-day timed event (day name for date, \"HH:mm\" for time value)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-18T14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\"]\"}]\nnow: \"2025-10-17T12:00:00\"\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Saturday\", \"value\": \"2025-10-18\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"John Doe\"}]\n\nExample R5: Next-week timed event (absolute date in \"Mon DD\" format)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-22T14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"addedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\"]\"}]\nnow: \"2025-10-17T12:00:00\"\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Oct 22\", \"value\": \"2025-10-22\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"John Doe\"}]\n\nExample R6: All-day event on same day (no date component, no time component)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"isAllDay\", \"value\": \"true\"}, {\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-17\"}]\nnow: \"2025-10-17T12:00:00\"\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"event\"}]\n\nExample R7: All-day event on next day (startDate with day name, no time components)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"isAllDay\", \"value\": \"true\"}, {\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-18\"}]\nnow: \"2025-10-17T12:00:00\"\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"event on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Saturday\", \"value\": \"2025-10-18\"}]\n\nExample R8: All-day event with absolute date (startDate with \"Mon DD\" format)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"isAllDay\", \"value\": \"true\"}, {\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2025-10-28\"}]\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"event on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Oct 28\", \"value\": \"2025-10-28\"}]\n\nExample R9: Multiple component types (recurrence, spatialTrigger, startDate, startTime)\nInput:\noriginal_components: []\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"title\", \"value\": \"Go to Grandmas\"}, {\"type\": \"start\", \"value\": \"2026-12-25T12:00\"}, {\"type\": \"recurrence\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY;INTERVAL=2\"}, {\"type\": \"spatialTrigger\", \"value\": \"{\"event\":\"leave\",\"location\":\"home\"}\"}, {\"type\": \"isAllDay\", \"value\": \"false\"}]\ntime_format: \"12h\"\nOutput:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Go to Grandmas on \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"Dec 25\", \"value\": \"2026-12-25\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"12pm\", \"value\": \"12:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"spatialTrigger\", \"text\": \"when I leave home\", \"value\": \"{\"event\":\"leave\",\"location\":\"home\"}\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"recurrenceRule\", \"text\": \"every other week\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY;INTERVAL=2\"}]\n\nExample 9: Removing participant from alias (converts back to individual when count ≤ 2)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"John and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Doe\",\"Joe John\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"removedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Joe John\"]\"}]\nOutput (converted back to individual participants - NEVER use \"John and 1 other\"):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting with \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"John\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" and \"}, {\"type\": \"participant\", \"text\": \"Jane\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\n\nExample 9b: Removing participant from alias (stays as alias when count ≥ 3)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"John and 3 others\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Doe\",\"Joe John\",\"Sarah Smith\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"removedParticipants\", \"value\": \"[\"Sarah Smith\"]\"}]\nOutput (stays as alias with updated count):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting with \"}, {\"type\": \"participantAlias\", \"text\": \"John and 2 others\", \"value\": \"[\"John Doe\",\"Jane Doe\",\"Joe John\"]\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\n\nExample 10: Changing timeZone\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"call at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"3pm\", \"value\": \"15:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"timeZone\", \"text\": \"Eastern\", \"value\": \"America/New_York\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"timeZone\", \"value\": \"America/Chicago\"}]\nOutput (timeZone text updated, NOT duplicated):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"call at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"3pm\", \"value\": \"15:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"timeZone\", \"text\": \"Central\", \"value\": \"America/Chicago\"}]\n\nExample 10b: Fixing malformed startTime that contains timezone text\nInput (MALFORMED - startTime incorrectly contains \"central\"):\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"dinner \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"feb 20\", \"value\": \"2026-02-20\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"6pm central\", \"value\": \"18:00\"}, {\"type\": \"timeZone\", \"text\": \"central\", \"value\": \"America/Chicago\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"timeZone\", \"value\": \"America/New_York\"}]\nOutput (FIXED - timezone removed from startTime, only in timeZone token):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"dinner \"}, {\"type\": \"startDate\", \"text\": \"feb 20\", \"value\": \"2026-02-20\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"6pm\", \"value\": \"18:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"timeZone\", \"text\": \"Eastern\", \"value\": \"America/New_York\"}]\n\nExample 11a: Recurrence pairing — original has no recurrenceRule, recurrence added (correction-only)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"recurrence\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY\"}]\nOutput (recurrenceRule placed after existing event-content tokens, with single-space connector):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}, {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \" \"}, {\"type\": \"recurrenceRule\", \"text\": \"every week\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY\"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: inserting \"with recurrence weekly\" or \"set to repeat every week\" — use the bare phrase\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: \"meeting every week at 2pm\" — recurrenceRule is tail-position; do not splice into the middle and do not restructure existing components\n\nExample 11b: Recurrence pairing — extending single-day to multi-day, \"every [day]\" style preserved (correction-only)\nInput:\noriginal_components: [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"dinner with friends \"}, {\"type\": \"recurrenceRule\", \"text\": \"every Thursday\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY;BYDAY=TH\"}]\nchanged_properties: [{\"type\": \"recurrence\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY;BYDAY=TH,FR\"}]\nOutput (singular day names retained after \"every\"):\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"dinner with friends \"}, {\"type\": \"recurrenceRule\", \"text\": \"every Thursday and Friday\", \"value\": \"FREQ=WEEKLY;BYDAY=TH,FR\"}]\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: \"every Thursdays and Fridays\" — never pluralize day names after \"every\"\nNOTE: ❌ WRONG: \"every Thursday and Fridays\" — never mix singular and plural\n\nOutput Format\n\nReturn ONLY a valid compact single-line JSON array of components. Do not include any explanation or\nadditional text. Output the entire JSON array on a single line with no unnecessary whitespace. Do not include any additional formatting outside of the JSON. Do not include backticks or \"json\" surrounding the JSON array.\n\nThe response must start immediately with [ and end with ] on a single line. Do not include any text before or after the JSON array. Use compact format with no indentation or line breaks within the JSON.\n\nExample valid response format:\n[{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"meeting at \"}, {\"type\": \"startTime\", \"text\": \"2pm\", \"value\": \"14:00\"}]"
